- **retention-time-alignment**: Use when after peak detection in untargeted metabolomics when you have identified ion signals across mass-to-charge and retention-time dimensions from replicate injections of the same samples, and you need to group peaks from different runs that represent the same metabolite before building a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-alignment))
- **retention-time-alignment**: Use when processing a batch of MRM mzML files (e.g. HILIC 1a/1b, MTBLS429, MTBLS897) whose metabolite peaks shift in RT between samples, and peak boundaries must be aligned before peak picking and ML-based peak reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-alignment--0b9ccb7416b4c64e))
- **retention-time-alignment**: Use when LC-MS targeted metabolomics EICs show retention-time drift between samples or replicate groups and the same isotopologue peak must be matched across samples before peak-area integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-alignment--a22229f5c215d397))
- **retention-time-alignment**: Use when two LC-MS feature lists — an in vitro prototype list and an in vivo metabolite list, each with retention time and accurate m/z per component — must be bridged by RT and m/z matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-alignment--45570cf96b413a9c))
- **retention-time-alignment-across-batches**: Use when you have extracted peaks from multiple LC/HRMS batches (n &gt; 500 samples across different analytical runs or days) and observe systematic retention time drift or offset between batches, preventing reliable cross-batch peak matching on m/z and RT alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-alignment-across-batches) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-alignment-and-feature-linking**: Use when multiple LC-MS/MS or DIA/SWATH maps must be made comparable — after feature detection and database searching, before protein inference — so peptide quantities refer to the same molecular feature in every run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-alignment-and-feature-linking))
- **retention-time-alignment-clustering**: Use when you have two or more nontargeted LCMS feature tables from the same analytical method and need to establish matched feature correspondences across datasets, then consolidate redundant features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-alignment-clustering))
- **retention-time-alignment-comparison**: Use when you have an MZmine-exported full-scan (MS1) feature table and a RegFilter-generated SPL .csv for the same LC-MS run and need to quantify what fraction of targeted precursor ions exist as DDA MS1 features or were actually fragmented in DDA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-alignment-comparison))
- **retention-time-alignment-correction**: Use when when processing a batch of centroided mzML or mzXML LC-MS raw data files where chromatographic retention times drift between sample acquisitions (common in large-scale metabolomics studies). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-alignment-correction))
- **retention-time-alignment-evaluation**: Use when you have run an NPP tool (XCMS, MZmine 2, etc.) on mzML files and need to assess whether the alignment stage preserved peak detection fidelity and isotopologue abundance ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-alignment-evaluation))
- **retention-time-alignment-index-mapping**: Use when when processing multiple LC-MS samples with varying scan numbers or retention-time drift, before constructing composite mass tracks for peak detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/retention-time-alignment-index-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-alignment-mapping-application**: Use when after mass tracks have been aligned across samples into a MassGrid structure and retention time calibration dictionaries (rt_cal_dict) have been computed for each sample, but before summing intensity vectors element-wise to construct the composite map. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-alignment-mapping-application))
- **retention-time-alignment-of-lc-ms-runs**: Use when multiple mzML runs (e.g. a spike-in series with randomized technical triplicates on an Acquity/Synapt HDMS instrument) show retention-time drift that would break feature correspondence prior to linking feature maps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-alignment-of-lc-ms-runs))
- **retention-time-alignment-parameters**: Use when preparing raw LC/MS mzXML data (e.g. MetaboLights MTBLS213) as an XCMS xcmsSet for isotopologue-pair detection with geoRge, where feature quality is determined by peak picking, alignment and grouping parameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-alignment-parameters))
- **retention-time-alignment-scoring**: Use when after feature m/z grouping and pairwise alignment detection have identified candidate feature pairs, and anchor points have been selected to establish retention time correspondence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-alignment-scoring))
- **retention-time-alignment-validation**: Use when after sample alignment step in untargeted LC-MS workflows, particularly when processing multi-sample cohorts with QC samples interspersed throughout the sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-alignment-validation) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-ambiguity-analysis**: Use when a metabolite standard or adduct in a metScribeR EIC review shows more than one manually labeled 'good' peak at different retention times, and ambiguous entries must be flagged or removed from the final RT/m/z library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-ambiguity-analysis))
- **retention-time-and-mass-based-sorting**: Use when you have raw GC-MS output in CSV format (with columns: Component.RT, Base.Peak.MZ, Component.Area, Compound.Name, Match.Factor, File. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-and-mass-based-sorting) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-and-mass-sorting-of-chromatographic-peaks**: Use when you have raw GC-MS output from Agilent Unknowns Analysis (a .CSV with columns Component.RT, Base.Peak.MZ, Component.Area, Compound.Name, Match.Factor, File. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-and-mass-sorting-of-chromatographic-peaks) · 3 copies)
- **retention-time-and-mz-based-alignment**: Use when after peak detection has been completed on individual LC-MS samples and you have a collection of detected peaks with m/z, retention time, and intensity values from each sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-and-mz-based-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-and-mz-weight-tuning**: Use when after anchor selection and RT mapping spline construction, when you have a fitted metabCombiner object with pre-aligned feature pair candidates and need to tune the scoring metric that combines retention time, m/z, and cosine similarity components. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-and-mz-weight-tuning))
- **retention-time-axis-construction**: Use when raw GCxGC-MS acquisition data arrives as a NetCDF (.cdf) file and you must build the two-dimensional retention-time axis (first dimension vs modulation-period second dimension) before smoothing, baseline correction, alignment, or MPCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-axis-construction))
- **retention-time-based-cluster-purity-evaluation**: Use when you need to assess how well a tool clusters MS/MS spectra from metabolomics datasets (qToF or Orbitrap, e.g. MSV000081981 or MSV000093033) without ground-truth identifications, or when benchmarking clustering purity against a database-search-based evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-based-cluster-purity-evaluation))
- **retention-time-based-feature-grouping**: Use when after chromatographic peak detection on preprocessed LC-MS data when you have an xcms result object (XcmsExperiment or xcmsSet) with detected peaks and need to collapse redundant m/z signals into feature groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-based-feature-grouping) · 3 copies)
- **retention-time-based-ion-association**: Use when when processing Data Dependent Acquisition (DDA) raw mass spectrometry data (mzML or mzXML format) and you need to deconvolute fragmentation spectra by matching precursor ions to their corresponding fragment ions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-based-ion-association) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-calibration-integration**: Use when you have LC-MS data from authentic standards run in positive and negative ESI modes, converted to .mzML format, and you need to build an in-house metabolite reference library for untargeted identification workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-calibration-integration))
- **retention-time-calibration-lowess-regression**: Use when after mass track construction and before composite map building, when you need to align retention times across multiple LC-MS samples. Trigger conditions: (1) you have identified high-selectivity landmark peaks (mSelectivity &gt; 0.99) in a reference sample; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-calibration-lowess-regression))
- **retention-time-calibration-via-lowess-regression**: Use when after mass track extraction and alignment across samples, when preparing to detect elution peaks on composite mass tracks. Use this when inter-sample retention time variation exceeds acceptable alignment tolerance (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-calibration-via-lowess-regression) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-candidate-filtering**: Use when a formula-based database retrieval (e.g. PubChem isomeric candidates by exact molecular formula, or MS-Finder candidate lists) must be narrowed using a measured liquid-chromatography retention time for the unknown molecule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-candidate-filtering))
- **retention-time-candidate-filtering-msfinder**: Use when MS-FINDER compound identification returns many isomeric candidate structures per formula and you have predicted RTs (Retip) plus experimental RTs to exclude candidates that are chromatographically implausible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-candidate-filtering-msfinder))
- **retention-time-chromatogram-parameterisation**: Use when building synthetic chemical datasets for ViMMS 2.0 simulations and the generated chemicals need controlled retention-time, intensity and chromatogram shapes (e.g. Gaussian peaks) alongside formula-derived m/z values, rather than chemicals extracted from existing mzML files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-chromatogram-parameterisation))
- **retention-time-clustering**: Use when you have an XCMS CentWave feature extraction output table containing multiple features with near-identical m/z values and retention times (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-clustering))
- **retention-time-clustering-alignment**: Use when you have extracted feature tables (via MS1 peak picking, MS2 recognition, or targeted list extraction) from two or more individual LC-MS samples and need to identify which features represent the same metabolite across samples before generating a unified, sample-aligned feature table for. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-clustering-alignment))
- **retention-time-co-elution-detection**: Use when after matching mass-to-charge ratios to a compound database (e.g., KEGG) and assigning adduct/fragment types, when you have an annotated feature table with retention times, m/z values, and intensity profiles across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-co-elution-detection))
- **retention-time-coarse-alignment**: Use when LC-MS feature tables from multiple runs show RT drift that must be removed before aligning features, as the preprocessing stage of DeepRTAlign's deep-learning correspondence workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-coarse-alignment))
- **retention-time-coelution-overlap-analysis**: Use when a large set of peptides with overlapping simulated/measured retention times is planned or simulated and you must predict chimeric-spectrum burden (2+ peptides selected for fragmentation simultaneously) before a real MS experiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-coelution-overlap-analysis))
- **retention-time-confirmation-against-standards**: Use when assigning highest-confidence (MSI level 1) compound identities from LC-MS/MS peak lists by matching precursor mass, retention time, and MS/MS fragments against an in-house authentic-standard spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-confirmation-against-standards))
- **retention-time-correction-and-alignment**: Use when you have multiple LC-MS runs with the same set of targets (compounds) and observe or expect retention time drift or jitter between runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-correction-and-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-correction-via-retention-index-markers**: Use when an untargeted LC/HRMS dataset (mzML/mzXML from hundreds of samples or multiple batches) shows systematic retention-time drift that must be removed before peak alignment or peak annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-correction-via-retention-index-markers))
- **retention-time-correlation-analysis**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics feature table contains multiple putative annotations per feature (shared monoisotopic mass and adduct variants) and a single most-probable annotation per feature must be selected using adduct probability, feature-intensity correlations, and mass error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-correlation-analysis))
- **retention-time-correlation-filtering**: Use when you have detected multiple LC-MS features (m/z peaks) across a chromatogram and need to distinguish true chemical relationships (isotopes differing by 1.003 Da, adducts with characteristic mass shifts, neutral loss fragments) from noise or unrelated peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-correlation-filtering))
- **retention-time-correlation-similarity-scoring**: Use when when you have detected multiple features from non-targeted mass spectrometry and need to group them by putative compound origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-correlation-similarity-scoring))
- **retention-time-database-construction**: Use when you need a reference set of predicted retention times for known metabolites (e.g. HMDB entries) to pair with m/z filtering and RT-based candidate ranking in untargeted metabolomics where MS/MS spectra are unavailable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-database-construction))
- **retention-time-descriptor-regression-modeling**: Use when you have SMILES/InChI structures or a compound list with measured LC retention times (HILIC or reversed-phase) and need RT predictions or descriptor-importance rankings to filter annotation candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-descriptor-regression-modeling))
- **retention-time-deviation-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify retention time stability of monitored compounds in a long-term QC pool (e.g. MetNexs) across LC-MS batches, comparing short-term within-batch deviations against interbatch offsets using QC4Metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-deviation-analysis))
- **retention-time-deviation-scoring-against-expected-rt**: Use when preprocessing LC-QQQ-MS MRM batch data (mzML) and you need to score candidate chromatographic peaks by their deviation from expected RT, including alignment-corrected deviation, for downstream ML peak classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-deviation-scoring-against-expected-rt))
- **retention-time-direction-filtering**: Use when TP components have been generated by patRoon componentization (parents linked to TP features) from LC-HRMS data in mixed positive/negative ionization, and you must prioritize which parent/TP pairs are chemically plausible by chromatographic retention-time direction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-direction-filtering))
- **retention-time-direction-matching**: Use when after componentization of parent and TP features with generateComponents(algorithm='tp'), when structural formula or compound annotations are available and you wish to prioritize TP candidates whose retention time behavior aligns with expected electronegativity, polarity, or hydrophobicity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-direction-matching))
- **retention-time-distance-clustering**: Use when GC–MS untargeted metabolomics samples have been deconvolved independently (e.g. via eRah's deconvolveComp) and the same compound must be matched across samples to produce one row per unique compound with per-sample areas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-distance-clustering))
- **retention-time-distribution-pattern-recognition**: Use when running semi-targeted Target Search over multiple batches of DDA-acquired LC-MS files (e.g., TripleTOF .wiff or QE HF .raw converted to Aird) and you need automated, consistent peak selection before manual inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-distribution-pattern-recognition))
- **retention-time-drift-alignment**: Use when targeted LC–MS peak integration shows RT shifts between runs and you have QC runs plus a target list containing housekeeping peaks whose RT deviations can anchor the alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-alignment))
- **retention-time-drift-correction**: Use when integrating multiple untargeted LC/MS serum studies (e.g., HILIC positive-mode mzML files) whose RT and intensity drifts from batch effects and run order prevent cross-study feature alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-correction--5a2cc00dadf7a5eb))
- **retention-time-drift-correction**: Use when MRM chromatographic peak lists from multiple samples show retention time shifts that must be corrected before peak alignment or EIC grouping, and per-sample RT drift values need to be estimated from chromatogram shapes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-correction--ab819a60f7da49a2))
- **retention-time-drift-correction**: Use when per-sample detected feature tables must be aligned to a reference sample and coordinates drift between acquisitions (e.g. LC-IMS-MS/MS runs from Bruker or Waters instruments). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-correction--35a5a24ba05678fc))
- **retention-time-drift-correction**: Use when per-sample LC/HRMS peaklists (m/z–RT pairs) from a multi-sample or multi-batch study show systematic RT drift and peaks must be aligned before cross-referencing or building an intensity matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-correction--facfd67fe8ee25fb))
- **retention-time-drift-correction-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing LC–MS feature alignment algorithms (e.g. G-Aligner, MZmine2, OpenMS, XCMS, local bipartite matching) on datasets with nonlinear RT drift, especially when a manually annotated feature list is available as ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-correction-benchmarking))
- **retention-time-drift-detection**: Use when historical or incoming LC-MS system suitability mzML files must be screened for batch-clustered retention time shifts or EIC patterns inconsistent with the intended chromatographic method (e.g., an F5 method run on a BEH Amide column) prior to data collection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-detection))
- **retention-time-drift-detection**: Use when you have LC-MS data processed through XCMS grouping that shows signs of RT drift (e.g., data acquired over extended periods or across many samples) and you suspect misalignment of feature groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-drift-detection))
- **retention-time-drift-registration**: Use when aligning features across multiple LC–MS runs whose RTs drift nonlinearly due to chromatogram condition changes (column temperature, aging, contamination), as the first stage before feature matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-registration))
- **retention-time-drift-threshold-logic**: Use when coding or auditing per-target QC decision rules for internal standards in untargeted LC-MS/MS runs processed by Rapid QC-MS, and the paper's 66%/80% fractional warning thresholds must be reproduced exactly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-threshold-logic))
- **retention-time-drift-time-m-z-querying**: Use when you have multidimensional MS data converted to MZA HDF5 format and need to retrieve specific spectra or chromatographic slices defined by one or more of: retention time (in minutes), ion mobility arrival time (in milliseconds for DT/SLIM or Vs/cm² for TimsTOF), or m/z value (as a float or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-drift-time-m-z-querying) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-drift-tolerance-setting**: Use when matching two untargeted LC–MS metabolomics feature tables whose RT, MZ, and FI values drift between experiments, and you must choose initial match thresholds before Step 2 uniqueness resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-drift-tolerance-setting))
- **retention-time-error-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating LC–MS RT prediction models (e.g. METLIN SMRT 25% hold-out molecules with experimental vs DLM-predicted RT in seconds) and you must report per-molecule relative errors and decide whether to exclude non-retained compounds (RT &lt; ~60 s). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-error-metric-computation))
- **retention-time-error-metric-extraction**: Decision support for comparing retention-time prediction accuracy across candidate models when multiple methods have been evaluated on the METLIN SMRT dataset and you need to rank mean/median relative error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-error-metric-extraction))
- **retention-time-error-threshold-optimization**: Use when candidate compounds from untargeted metabolomics have RT-Transformer-predicted vs observed RTs and you must choose an RT-error cutoff to discard false identities without losing true ones. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-error-threshold-optimization))
- **retention-time-extraction-from-raw-spectra**: Use when when you have Thermo Orbitrap .raw files containing known reference peptides (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-extraction-from-raw-spectra) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-feature-clustering**: Use when after chromatographic peak detection (findChromPeaks) when you have a processed XcmsExperiment object with detected peaks and need to perform initial feature grouping. Use it when features of the same compound are expected to co-elute within a narrow retention-time window (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-feature-clustering) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-feature-distribution-analysis**: Use when you have extracted retention times from top MS1 features in an LC-MS/MS experiment and need to assess whether the gradient configuration (start and end time in minutes) achieves adequate compound separation across the full chemical space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-feature-distribution-analysis))
- **retention-time-feature-engineering**: Use when building molecular feature inputs (descriptors and/or fingerprints) for RT regressors on compounds with SMILES/SDF structures and RT labels, and you must handle missing descriptors and collinear features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-feature-engineering))
- **retention-time-filtered-annotation-ranking**: Use when you have m/z-matched candidate annotation lists (e.g. from LipidBlast or similar) plus measured retention times, and you need to improve Recall@1/Recall@k of the correct identification by adding orthogonal RT or CCS evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-filtered-annotation-ranking))
- **retention-time-filtering**: Use when working with GCIMS datasets where retention time spans a wide range (e.g., 0–1500 s) but your analytes of interest are confined to a narrower window (e.g., 0–1100 s). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **retention-time-filtration**: Use when a CAMERA/XCMS LC/MS feature list contains early-eluting contaminants (e.g. under the 45 s exclusion used for Arabidopsis rosette extracts) or when you must bound candidate metabolites by RT and accommodate retention-time shifts caused by isotope labeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-filtration))
- **retention-time-fwhm-window-matching**: Use when you have a peak table (Compound Discoverer 3.3 .cdResult or .csv) with a mix of lipid-identified and unidentified features and need to classify unidentified features as adducts, in-source fragments, or dimers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-fwhm-window-matching))
- **retention-time-index-calibration**: Use when you have acquired a bottom-up proteomics LC-MS/MS run (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-index-calibration))
- **retention-time-indexed-spectral-aggregation**: Use when you have Thermo Fisher Scientific .raw files from an LC-MS experiment and need to extract spectral features (base-peak m/z, intensity, scan-level properties) indexed by retention time for downstream statistical analysis, method optimization, or diagnostic visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-indexed-spectral-aggregation))
- **retention-time-integer-quantization**: Use when converting vendor MRM chromatogram files (e.g. from Xcalibur or SCIEX OS) into the Aird format and storage size or web-side rendering speed for hundreds of chromatograms is the bottleneck. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-integer-quantization))
- **retention-time-intensity-data-extraction**: Use when you have xcms-processed LC-MS data with detected misaligned feature groups and need to recover the underlying raw retention time–intensity profiles for each feature and sample combination prior to realignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-intensity-data-extraction))
- **retention-time-intensity-extraction**: Use when when you have imported mass spectrometry data in .raw, .d, or mzXML format and need to generate a TIC plot to visualize overall sample ionization intensity as a function of retention time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-intensity-extraction))
- **retention-time-intensity-tabulation**: Use when when you have a resolved mzML or mzXML spectrum file and need to visualize or analyze the temporal intensity profile of a specific analyte (defined by its m/z value). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-intensity-tabulation))
- **retention-time-interval-encoding**: Use when converting the retention times of top MS1 signals (e.g., from BAGO's findTopSignals on mzML/mzXML data) into a bounded separation-efficiency score to serve as the objective for LC gradient optimization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-interval-encoding))
- **retention-time-library-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify how faithfully an automated LC-MS peak-quality filter reproduces manual quality labeling of standard peaks, using precision, recall, F1, and misclassification counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-library-benchmarking))
- **retention-time-logp-modeling**: Use when LC/MS HR-MS/MS candidates must be re-ranked using measured retention times on a reverse-phase column and reference-standard RTs are available to fit the log P correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-logp-modeling))
- **retention-time-map-alignment**: Use when multiple centroided LC-MS mzML maps must be brought onto a common retention-time scale (e.g. before FeatureLinkerUnlabeledKD consensus grouping) and RT drift between runs would break cross-sample feature matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-map-alignment))
- **retention-time-mapping-spline-fitting**: Use when after anchor feature pairs (m/z and retention time values) have been selected from two disparately-acquired LC-MS datasets, and you need to correct for systematic retention time differences between the datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mapping-spline-fitting))
- **retention-time-mass-alignment**: Use when you have two independent LC-MS untargeted metabolomic feature datasets (each with retention time and m/z values) and need to identify which features in one dataset correspond to features in the other. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/retention-time-mass-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-mass-correspondence-resolution**: Use when you have two LC-MS untargeted metabolomic feature tables (each containing m/z, retention time, and intensity columns) and need to establish which features in dataset A correspond to which features in dataset B, typically for comparative metabolomics, batch effect correction, or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mass-correspondence-resolution))
- **retention-time-mass-proximity-matching**: Use when after sample alignment and grouping of isotopologues and adducts have been completed, when the aligned feature table contains NA or zero entries (missing intensities) for features that are detected in some samples but fall below the detection threshold in others. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mass-proximity-matching) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-mass-tolerance-calibration**: Use when you have multiple feature tables (CSV files) from different LC-MS analytical experiments, each containing mass, retention time, intensity, isotope, and adduct annotations, and you need to merge them into a single aligned feature matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mass-tolerance-calibration))
- **retention-time-misalignment-detection**: Use when you have two XCMS grouping results (one with an RT window near the RT sampling resolution, one with the expected maximal RT drift) and need to identify which peak groups are misaligned before realignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-misalignment-detection))
- **retention-time-model-filtering**: Use when lipid identifications from the LipiDex 2 Peak Finder and Spectral Searcher pipeline need QC filtering based on retention-time plausibility, i.e. removing identifications whose measured RT deviates substantially from the ECN-predicted RT for their lipid class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-model-filtering))
- **retention-time-mz-alignment**: Use when processing raw LC/MS data (mzML or mzXML format) from multi-sample cohorts where retention time or intensity drift is suspected due to batch effects, instrument calibration drift, or variable run order. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mz-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-mz-alignment-matching**: Use when you have extracted feature tables (m/z, intensity, retention time) from multiple breath samples (mzML or mzXML files) via feature extraction, and need to identify which features represent the same volatile organic compound (VOC) across samples to enable cross-sample intensity comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mz-alignment-matching))
- **retention-time-mz-coordinate-mapping**: Use when when you have mass-spectrometry data in tabular form (Pandas DataFrame) with columns for m/z, retention time or ion mobility, and intensity, and you need to visualize the 2D distribution of peaks to assess peak separation, detect co-elution, or examine chromatographic and mass resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mz-coordinate-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-mz-feature-extraction**: Use when you have LC-HRMS profile-mode data (e.g., netCDF or mzML format) and need to convert detected or reference chromatographic peaks into fixed-size 2D arrays (rt × mz regions) to train or apply a convolutional neural network for peak classification and bounding-box prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mz-feature-extraction))
- **retention-time-mz-heatmap-rendering**: Use when you hold an on-disk raw MS file (e.g. the TMT PXD000001 run from a Thermo Orbitrap Q Exactive) and need exploratory visual inspection: ion-intensity heat maps over retention time and m/z, 3D scan maps, or a zoom on a peptide's isotopic envelope. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/retention-time-mz-heatmap-rendering))
- **retention-time-mz-indexing**: Use when when you have parsed .mzML or Thermo .raw LC-MS data and need to support interactive or programmatic queries by retention time (RT) and mass-to-charge ratio (m/z) without re-scanning the entire file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mz-indexing))
- **retention-time-mz-intensity-mapping**: Use when you have processed LC-MS run data (feature table or peak detection output) containing internal standard identifications with retention times, m/z values, and intensity measurements across multiple samples, and you need to rapidly detect instrumental drift, retention time shifts, or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-mz-intensity-mapping))
- **retention-time-mz-matrix-resampling**: Use when raw profile-mode mzXML/mzML LC-HRMS chromatograms must be converted into fixed-size (rt x mz) candidate-peak areas before CNN-based peak-apex classification, e.g. as the preprocessing step of PeakBot peak picking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-mz-matrix-resampling))
- **retention-time-mz-scatter-visualization**: Use when a scored NP Analyst feature network (nodes = MS features/samples, edges = bioactivity-weighted associations) needs to be divided into communities and rendered as network graphML, rt vs m/z scatter plots, and community views for prioritizing bioactive MS features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-mz-scatter-visualization))
- **retention-time-normalization-and-scoring**: Use when you have XCMS-aligned feature tables with retention time values and need to compute pairwise feature similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-normalization-and-scoring))
- **retention-time-order-validation**: Use when a candidate PFAS homologous series (features grouped by matching CF2-normalized Kendrick mass defect and CF2-unit mass differences) needs confirmation that chromatographic retention time follows the expected order along the series. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-order-validation))
- **retention-time-pairing-from-adjacent-chromatographic-peaks**: Use when building pairwise training data from a reference retention-time dataset (e.g., 'dataset_83') where ordered molecule pairs (Ci eluting before Cj) are needed to train a ranking model like RankNet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-pairing-from-adjacent-chromatographic-peaks))
- **retention-time-peak-assignment-per-formula**: Use when confirmed LC-IM-MS peak detections must be linked to specific molecular formulae with assigned RT values, e.g. after peak picking mzML-derived ion traces and before building SQL-format database entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-peak-assignment-per-formula))
- **retention-time-peak-boundary-assessment**: Use when building an LC-MS metabolite RT library from mzML files of authentic standards and you must decide which detected EIC peaks have valid boundaries and RTs worth exporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-peak-boundary-assessment))
- **retention-time-peak-flagging**: Use when after applying peak detection algorithms to identify local maxima in feature signals across retention time or m/z dimensions, but before exporting or filtering the peak list for further analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-peak-flagging))
- **retention-time-peak-matching**: Use when after drift correction and quality flagging, when you have a feature abundance matrix with associated metadata (Feature_ID, m/z, retention time) and need to identify which features likely represent the same underlying metabolite or adduct series before statistical analysis or metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-peak-matching))
- **retention-time-peak-picking**: Use when a targeted, formula-driven workflow needs to extract monoisotopic primary-ion m/z traces (optionally multiply charged) from LC-IM-MS mzML data and pick chromatographic peaks in the RT dimension, rather than performing untargeted feature detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-peak-picking))
- **retention-time-piecewise-linear-calibration**: Use when lipid features were separated on a modified HILIC gradient or a non-reference Kinetex HILIC column (e.g. 50 mm or 30 mm instead of 100 mm), and their measured RTs must be mapped to the reference database RTs (23 lipid classes carry HILIC RTs) before identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-piecewise-linear-calibration))
- **retention-time-precision-statistics**: Use when system suitability testing must report chromatographic reproducibility: retention times of SST standard compounds are available per acquisition file across multiple runs, platforms, or operators, and RSD summaries are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-precision-statistics))
- **retention-time-prediction**: Use when you have MS1-formatted mass spectrometry files from a liquid chromatography–mass spectrometry (LC-MS) experiment and need to predict the retention time of peptide ions without relying on spectral libraries, empirical models, or manual feature engineering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-prediction))
- **retention-time-prediction**: Use when you have a set of small-molecule structures (SMILES, InChI, or SDF format) and need to predict their chromatographic retention times for a specific method, either to support metabolite annotation, validate experimental assignments, or project RTs across different chromatographic platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/retention-time-prediction))
- **retention-time-prediction-chromatography**: Use when you have a new chromatographic dataset with molecular structures (as InChI or SMILES) and experimentally measured retention times, and you want to predict retention times for unannotated metabolites or validate predictions on a held-out test set without retraining from scratch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-prediction-chromatography))
- **retention-time-prediction-error-analysis**: Use when you have true vs. predicted retention times for a fixed test set of molecules (e.g. SMRT) and need error-distribution coverage statistics and a ±2 SD exclusion-threshold false negative rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-error-analysis--935bec607fc22d0a))
- **retention-time-prediction-error-analysis**: Use when you have a trained RT prediction model (e.g., a DLM on the METLIN SMRT dataset) plus predicted-vs-experimental RT for a validation split, and want to test whether structurally similar training-set counterparts reduce prediction error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-error-analysis--f13f403d6dd3d300))
- **retention-time-prediction-feature-evaluation**: Decision support for choosing or validating a retention time predictor (e.g. DeepLC, Prosit-derived RT, RTPredict) whose ΔRT error will serve as a rescoring feature in Percolator-style PSM rescoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-feature-evaluation))
- **retention-time-prediction-feature-generation**: Use when rescoring immunopeptidomics PSMs with Percolator and a sequence-based retention-time feature is needed: fine-tune AutoRT base models per LC-MS/MS run, predict RT for each PSM, and compute RTdelta = |RTobserved - RTpredicted|. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/retention-time-prediction-feature-generation))
- **retention-time-prediction-fine-tuning**: Use when predicting liquid chromatography retention times for a new chromatographic method from molecular structures (InChI/SMILES), especially with limited labeled data where training a retention-time model from scratch is infeasible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-fine-tuning))
- **retention-time-prediction-from-chemical-descriptors**: Use when SMILES structures need predicted retention times (HILIC or RPLC) to rank or filter candidate structures in untargeted metabolomics, or when batch-predicting RTs for a large compound database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-from-chemical-descriptors))
- **retention-time-prediction-from-structures**: Use when you have molecular structures (SMILES or SDF format) for which you need to predict retention time in liquid chromatography, especially when your target dataset contains fewer than ~500 annotated examples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-prediction-from-structures))
- **retention-time-prediction-inference**: Use when you have a molecular formula (e.g. from PubChem PUG REST) with multiple isomeric candidate SMILES and one observed retention time under an SMRT-type chromatographic method, and need to prune inconsistent candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-inference))
- **retention-time-prediction-integration**: Use when building multidimensional (4D) LC-IM-MS/MS identification databases where computationally predicted retention times must be supplied for candidate molecules lacking experimental RT measurements, e.g. derivatized sterol lipid entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-integration))
- **retention-time-prediction-model-inference**: Use when you have SMILES/InChI structures or a compound descriptor table and need predicted retention times for HILIC or reversed-phase LC-MS runs to filter isomer candidates, rank unknowns, or remove unlikely annotations in metabolomics workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-model-inference))
- **retention-time-prediction-modeling**: Use when you have a set of small-molecule compounds (e.g., from MS/MS library matching or database annotation) that require retention time validation or ranking to resolve ambiguous identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-prediction-modeling) · 4 copies)
- **retention-time-prediction-modeling**: Use when you need to predict retention times for compounds from SMILES/structures to complement MS/MS spectral matching, or to benchmark a learned RT predictor against a logP-based linear regression baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prediction-modeling))
- **retention-time-prediction-optimization**: Use when when you have a retention-time dataset (e.g., SMRT or Eawag_XBridgeC18_364) in .xlsx format and need to train or adapt a graph neural network model to predict chromatographic retention times for new compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-prediction-optimization) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-prediction-scoring**: Use when you have a list of candidate metabolites for an unknown compound (from mass-to-structure search or library matching), experimental retention time(s) from one or more chromatographic methods, and access to a trained DNN RT predictor and meta-learned RT projection model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-prediction-scoring))
- **retention-time-prediction-transfer-learning**: Use when analyzing LC-MS/MS immunopeptidomics PSMs where a retention-time-derived feature (RTdelta) should be added to Percolator rescoring, and run-specific RT prediction is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/retention-time-prediction-transfer-learning))
- **retention-time-prediction-validation**: Use when after training a GNN-RT model on preprocessed molecular graph data (from Train.py) or after applying transfer learning to an in-house dataset (from Transferlearning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-prediction-validation))
- **retention-time-prior-integration**: Use when the MS1 database contains RT entries (e.g. '30;60' elution ranges) for compounds, and LC-MS feature annotations must be filtered or reweighted by whether the feature's measured retention time falls within that expected range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-prior-integration))
- **retention-time-projection-across-chromatographic-methods**: Use when when you have retention times measured on one chromatographic method and need to predict or map them to another method with minimal or no overlap in measured molecules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-projection-across-chromatographic-methods) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-projection-across-chromatographic-methods**: Use when you have deep-learning-predicted RTs on one chromatographic method and need them transformed onto the time scale of other experimental reverse-phase methods (ECMs) to compute projection errors or rank candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-projection-across-chromatographic-methods))
- **retention-time-projection-between-chromatographic-methods**: Use when predicted RTs from a machine learning model must be projected to a specific chromatographic method for which only ~10 experimentally identified molecules (predicted-vs-experimental RT pairs) are available, and confidence intervals on the projections are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-projection-between-chromatographic-methods))
- **retention-time-projection-meta-learning**: Use when you have a small set (10–50 molecules) of paired predicted/experimental RTs for a target chromatographic method and need to map DNN-predicted RTs onto that method's scale with 95% prediction intervals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-projection-meta-learning))
- **retention-time-projection-regression**: Use when you have DLM-predicted RTs, a molecule library with accurate masses (e.g. HMDB), and experimental RTs from a target chromatographic method, and you need to rank/filter candidate identities from 10 ppm accurate-mass search results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-projection-regression))
- **retention-time-range-configuration-for-virtual-runs**: Use when executing a simulated LC-MS/MS acquisition in ViMMS 2.0 and you must bind the mass spectrometer and controller in an Environment and define the simulated RT window before env.run(). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-range-configuration-for-virtual-runs))
- **retention-time-range-filtering**: Use when you have raw IM-MS data (Agilent MassHunter .d or UIMF format) and need to exclude early or late chromatographic regions—e.g., to skip dead volume, exclude blank runs, focus on a known analyte window, or reduce file size for faster processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-range-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-reference-library-construction**: Use when building a metabolite reference library from chemical standard injections in LC-MS: replicate mzXML/mzML files of standard mixtures are available and validated retention times per analyte must be extracted and tabulated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-reference-library-construction))
- **retention-time-regression-model-training**: Use when experimental retention times are available for a compound library (e.g. 880 HILIC compounds from MoNA or 398 RPLC compounds from RIKEN PlaSMA) and the goal is a descriptor-based RT prediction model to filter false-positive annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-regression-model-training--3d019a2f36d569cf))
- **retention-time-regression-model-training**: Use when you have small-molecule structures with measured LC retention times (e.g. a METLIN SMRT-style dataset) and need an accurate data-driven retention time regression model for structural identification support. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-regression-model-training--8849e07a1d6b4a9c))
- **retention-time-regression-modeling**: Use when authentic standards are unavailable and LC-HRMS annotations (e.g. CMDN-annotated metabolites with accurate m/z and RT) need chromatographic plausibility checks by predicting RT from SMILES/InChIKey structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-regression-modeling))
- **retention-time-regression-output-specification**: Use when after initializing and executing a forward pass through a dual-branch RT-Transformer model (combining fingerprint and molecular graph inputs) on a batch of molecular samples, to verify that the output tensor conforms to the expected shape, data type, and numeric range for retention time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-regression-output-specification))
- **retention-time-regression-training**: Use when you have a large set of molecules with experimental retention times (e.g., SMRT reversed-phase LC-MS data) plus alvaDesc fingerprints/descriptors and need to train or compare RT prediction regressors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-regression-training))
- **retention-time-rsd-computation-across-replicates**: Use when validating LC-MS reference analytes from &gt;=3 replicate runs (e.g. HILIC standard mixtures in mzML/mzXML) and you must quantify retention-time reproducibility per analyte against a user-specified threshold c (default 15%). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-rsd-computation-across-replicates))
- **retention-time-scan-mapping**: Use when when you have loaded an LC-MS spectrum file (mzML, mzXML, or equivalent) into the GNPS LCMS Visualization Dashboard and need to annotate extracted ion chromatograms with the precise retention time or scan ID positions where MS2 events occurred. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-scan-mapping))
- **retention-time-scheduled-method-export**: Use when a second-round targeted MS method (SRM/MRM) must be built from measured retention times observed in a first-pass Skyline analysis, e.g. refining an arginine/polyamine pathway assay after initial unscheduled acquisition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-scheduled-method-export))
- **retention-time-score-evaluation**: Use when scoring metabolite annotations from MS/MS spectra in negative mode where retention time is available, e.g. verifying that a candidate like 1-O-beta-D-glucopyranosyl sinapate at RT 1.27 min receives the expected RT score of 1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-score-evaluation))
- **retention-time-search-frame-restriction**: Use when re-detecting peaks from LC-HRMS mzXML chromatograms for a known reference feature list, and narrowed m/z + retention-time search windows are available or can be derived per feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-search-frame-restriction))
- **retention-time-shift-and-noise-thresholding**: Use when a targeted or suspect HRMS prescreening run has produced EICs and MS2 scans per compound and you must pass/fail each parent point against experimental m/z error, RT error, and MS1/MS2 intensity thresholds before further identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-shift-and-noise-thresholding))
- **retention-time-shift-correction**: Use when aligning XCMS-derived feature lists or target chromatograms from different LC-HRMS batches where retention times are shifted (e.g. 7–52 s or -9 to -24 s ranges) and m/z deviates slightly (e.g. -3.4 ppm) relative to a reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-shift-correction))
- **retention-time-shift-correction-isotope-labelling**: Use when isotope-labelling experiments (deuterium- or 13C-labelled substrates) shift chromatographic retention times, and m/z-difference-based substrate-metabolite discovery would otherwise miss the labelled peak pair. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-shift-correction-isotope-labelling))
- **retention-time-shift-interpretation**: Use when annotating reaction-derived xenobiotic metabolites from LC-HRMS feature tables and you need to attach RT-shift metadata and biotransformation labels to metabolite network nodes, or use RT consistency as a validation channel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-shift-interpretation))
- **retention-time-shift-modeling**: Use when two untargeted LC–MS metabolomics feature tables (samples × features with RT, m/z, and FI medians per feature) must be matched feature-by-feature despite inter-dataset retention-time shifts, e.g. for batch combination or multicohort integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-shift-modeling))
- **retention-time-shift-quantification**: Use when a sequence of LC-MS/MS acquisitions must be monitored for RT drift, e.g. to confirm each specimen's RT against IS/RS/ES m/z-RT windows written live by the Python Watchdog watcher. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-shift-quantification))
- **retention-time-shift-simulation**: Use when you need to stress-test an LC-MS RT alignment tool against ground-truth correspondences, or to determine at what RT-shift variability an aligner's precision and recall degrade. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-shift-simulation))
- **retention-time-spline-fitting**: Use when a pair of untargeted LC-MS metabolomics data sets acquired under different gradient elution methods must be combined and RT drift must be corrected before ranking feature pair alignments by mapped retention time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-spline-fitting))
- **retention-time-stability-assessment**: Use when a metabolomics/lipidomics feature table contains repeated pooled-QC injections and you need to verify retention time precision (RSD &lt; 2%) before trusting chromatographic alignment across the sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-stability-assessment))
- **retention-time-tolerance-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when peak lists from multiple LC-MS samples have been aligned with configurable retention-time (RT) tolerance parameters and you need to test whether the aligner's precision/recall holds across tolerance settings and feature-row counts, or to identify dataset fractions where one aligner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-tolerance-sensitivity-analysis))
- **retention-time-tolerance-sweep**: Use when comparing peak-list alignment algorithms on LC-MS peak lists with known ground-truth correspondences, and you need a tolerance sweep (e.g., 5/20/50 s RT) scored by precision and recall. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-tolerance-sweep))
- **retention-time-trend-chain-extension**: Use when running untargeted homologue series detection on LC-MS peak tables and candidate series have reached three members, so their RT step trends must be learned to constrain further chain extension. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-trend-chain-extension))
- **retention-time-warping-function-substitution**: Use when XCMS grouping has flagged features as misaligned and ncGTW has produced per-sample warping functions, and you want more accurate peak-filling (integration of missing peaks) than the default single-warping-function XCMS alignment provides. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-warping-function-substitution))
- **retention-time-window-aggregation**: Use when raw LC-MS profile spectra must be converted to a fixed-size 2D intensity matrix (m/z x RT windows) as input to CNN/LSTM/Transformer classifiers, and the m/z and RT dimensions vary between acquisitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-window-aggregation))
- **retention-time-window-based-peak-grouping**: Use when processing LC–MS/MS mzXML files of released N-glycans where multiple peaks share the same m/z and need to be separated and counted as distinct isomer peaks within a fixed retention-time window. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-window-based-peak-grouping))
- **retention-time-window-clustering**: Use when after filtering LC-MS features by statistical significance (e.g., p-value &lt; 0.01) and you need to link individual m/z features into structural clusters representing the same metabolite in different ionization states or isotopic forms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-window-clustering) · 2 copies)
- **retention-time-window-encoding**: Use when converting MZmine 2 aligned peak groups (m/z and retention-time windows plus per-sample peak shapes) into the standardized 64x64 pixel raster images that feed the image-based deep neural network for true/false peak classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-window-encoding))
- **retention-time-window-extraction**: Use when confirming a candidate glycan identified in MS1 by pulling the MS2 fragmentation scans in a narrow window around the glycan's MS1 retention time (MS1 RT minus 0.1 min) from an mzXML-derived LC-MS/MS run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-window-extraction))
- **retention-time-window-extraction**: Use when you have loaded sqMass files containing pre-extracted transition group chromatograms and need to isolate chromatographic traces within a specific retention time interval—either defined by OpenSwath feature metadata (apex retention time ± margin) or by manual user selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-window-extraction))
- **retention-time-window-filtering**: Use when you have mzML/mzXML chromatogram files from Thermo, Waters, or Bruker instruments and need to extract MS1 and MS2 scans matching both a target m/z value AND a known or suspected retention time range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-window-filtering))
- **retention-time-window-filtering**: Use when a raw LC-IM-MS dataset has been converted to .mza and the analysis target is a narrow chromatographic region (e.g., a 0.5 min RT window) whose spectra must be isolated before mobility or m/z visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-window-filtering))
- **retention-time-window-matching**: Use when you have an Agilent Unknowns Analysis GC-MS hit table with Component.RT, Component.Area, Base.Peak.MZ, File.Name, Compound.Name, and Match.Factor columns and need per-sample areas for user-specified chemicals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-window-matching--be4a8960033b3189))
- **retention-time-window-matching**: Use when evaluating MS/MS spectral clustering (msCluster, Falcon, MaRaCluster) on metabolomics or proteomics LC-MS data that lacks annotated peptide/molecule labels, by exploiting precursor m/z and RT consistency instead of database identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-window-matching--3be54cd468303a2a))
- **retention-time-window-pair-filtering**: Use when a non-targeted LC-MS peak table (e.g. MS-DIAL export) needs same-metabolite feature grouping, and you must constrain which feature pairs are considered correlated by a retention-time window before graph clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-window-pair-filtering))
- **retention-time-window-tolerance-checking**: Use when you have acquired LC-MS data and need to verify run quality before proceeding to metabolite identification or quantification. Retention time checking is essential when: (1) you have established expected retention time ranges for known internal standards or reference compounds; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-window-tolerance-checking))
- **retention-time-windowing**: Use when you have a complete TIC (total ion current) table indexed by retention time and need to visualize or analyze only a specific time interval—for example, when isolating a chromatographic peak region (9–10 min) before plotting or when a mass spectrometry run spans a time range wider than the. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/retention-time-windowing))
- **retention-time-zscore-annotation-ranking**: Use when annotating small molecules with only m/z and retention times (no MS/MS spectra), and you need to rank a mass-filtered candidate list using RT predictions projected to your chromatographic method from ~10 identified molecules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/retention-time-zscore-annotation-ranking))
- **retention-toolkit**: Script and QA any short/long video for retention — the 30 scroll-stop/re-hook techniques (Ava/thebranding.ai carousel), Context→Challenge→Transition openers, Hook→Retain→Reward→Open-loop spine (Caleb Ralston × Hormozi study), beat maps by length, and a retention-curve QA. Use BEFORE writing any hook/script/edit plan for the user's personal brand or Alwazour reels. Extends social-growth-science and marketing-psychology; never fabricates facts to make a hook work. ([Zuhair-01/claude-skills-and-systems](https://github.com/Zuhair-01/claude-skills-and-systems/tree/HEAD/skills/retention-toolkit) · ★2)
- **retention-tracker**: Track construction retainage/retention amounts. Monitor held amounts by subcontractor, track release conditions, and manage retainage billing. ([datadrivenconstruction/DDC_Skills_for_AI_Agents_in_Construction](https://github.com/datadrivenconstruction/DDC_Skills_for_AI_Agents_in_Construction/tree/HEAD/4_DDC_Curated/Financial-Management/retention-tracker) · ★429 · 8 copies)
- **retention-video-build**: This skill should be used when the user asks to BUILD or RENDER a vertical short-form video — "make a Reels/TikTok/YouTube Shorts video", "build a short-form video template", "edit for retention", "add pattern interrupts", "make a video loop", "fix the first 3 seconds", "pace cuts for a vertical video", or "set up 9:16 safe areas". Covers the hook→retention→loop grammar of vertical short-form and the code that produces the file (distinct from a launch hype film). For writing the script, hook copy, CTA, and posting plan before anything is built, use the `social` skill instead. ([Jo5i4swastaken/Skills-Locker](https://github.com/Jo5i4swastaken/Skills-Locker/tree/HEAD/skills/retention-video-build))
- **retention-voc**: Mine account-level voice-of-customer themes from churn, at-risk, QBR, renewal, expansion, and cancellation evidence. Use to extract the qualitative retention narrative behind churn and feed retention themes to the roadmap. The customer-success VoC lens — account-level, distinct from individual-user VoC. ([hpsgd/turtlestack](https://github.com/hpsgd/turtlestack/tree/HEAD/plugins/product/customer-success/skills/retention-voc))
- **retention-watch**: Spots at-risk policyholder accounts — non-payment, complaints, or no recent contact — and suggests a retention action for each. Aggregates AMS/CRM notes, billing status, and email sentiment into a ranked watch-list. Use when the user asks which accounts are at risk, about retention, non-payment, or what's likely to lapse for reasons other than a scheduled renewal. ([Attri-Inc/attri-insurance-plugin](https://github.com/Attri-Inc/attri-insurance-plugin/tree/HEAD/attri-insurance/skills/retention-watch))
- **retention-workshop**: Teach retention strategy through guided exploration.
